INTERCUT TRAILER #1: The Altar Betrayal Speedrun (Intra-Episode Jumpcut)
========================================================================
Project / Show   : I Married a Broken Soldier. He was a Duke
Source Episode(s): Episode 1
Intercut         : Yes — Multi-Segment (Intra-Episode) (2 segments)
Total Duration   : 47.2s
Score            : 96.5/100
Marketing Tier   : Tier 1: Top-of-Funnel (Cold UA) - High Pacing

INTERCUT SEGMENTS (IN PLAYBACK ORDER):
  Segment 1: Episode 1 | 00:11.30 -> 00:25.50 (14.2s)
    Beat: Matriarch blocks bride at church doors: 'You should turn around, Juliana... Gerald is waiting for me... Not anymore!'
  Segment 2: Episode 1 | 01:45.00 -> 02:18.00 (33.0s)
    Beat: Groom at altar: 'I cannot marry a woman of such plainness... when Clarissa is the true jewel of the county.' Drone swells to cliffhanger peak, cut to black.

0-3s Hook        : Matriarch blocks the bride at the church threshold, sneering that the groom is no longer waiting for her.
Cliffhanger Cut  : Groom publicly discards Juliana for her sister before the entire congregation, cutting to black on an ominous drone swell.
Why It Works     : Bypasses 60 seconds of walking exposition to connect the initial shock directly to the altar rejection, doubling pacing.

========================================================================

Source Episode(s): Episode 3, Episode 4
Intercut         : Yes — Multi-Segment (Cross-Episode) (2 segments)

    Beat: Stranger kneels on cathedral aisle: 'You came here to become a wife. I am offering it back. Lady Juliana, accept my hand.'
  Segment 2: Episode 4 | 00:00.00 -> 00:27.70 (27.7s)
    Beat: Cathedral erupts in shock. Gerald mocks him as a cripple. Juliana asks: 'Why would you offer me your name?'

0-3s Hook        : War hero drops to one knee offering marriage to the bride who was just abandoned minutes earlier.
Cliffhanger Cut  : Cathedral gasps as the discarded bride asks the mysterious war hero: 'Why would you offer me your name?'
Why It Works     : Captures the single most viral trope in microdrama history (humiliated bride marries high-status protector).
